{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UzEQ9wpPhuxTNRFUeVYLJb","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator – System Architecture  \n     3→_Author: Core Platform Team_  \n     4→_Last updated: 2024-05-30_\n     5→\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→## 1  Overview\n     9→\n    10→LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator (repo root: `ml_nlp/lexilearn_orchestrator`) is a **modular, C-based, MVC platform** for large-scale, adaptive language-learning applications.  \n    11→The orchestrator stitches together Machine-Learning Ops essentials—hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, automated retraining—behind a clean Controller façade exposing a single public header `lexilearn.h`.\n    12→\n    13→Key design drivers:\n    14→\n    15→* **High throughput** ingestion of multimodal classroom data.\n    16→* **Pluggable NLP/ML strategies** switchable at run-time.\n    17→* **Deterministic** builds, reproducible experiments, and strong type-safety.\n    18→* **Embeddability** in web servers, desktop LMS plug-ins, and edge devices.\n    19→\n    20→---\n    21→\n    22→## 2  Top-Level Component Diagram\n    23→\n    24→```text\n    25→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n    26→│                            Application Layer                           │\n    27→│ ┌────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐                 │\n    28→│ │  REST API  │    │ CLI Pipeline │    │  Batch Jobs  │ <─┐             │\n    29→│ └────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘   │ Application │\n    30→└──────│───────────────────│───────────────────│───────────┘             │\n    31→       ▼                   ▼                   ▼                         ▼\n    32→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n    33→│                           MVC Orchestrator                             │\n    34→│ ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐                   │\n    35→│ │  View    │◀──────▶│ Controller│◀──────▶│  Model   │                   │\n    36→│ └──────────┘        └──────────┘        └──────────┘                   │\n    37→│  │                       ▲                   │                         │\n    38→│  │ Observer Pattern      │ Factory Pattern   │ Strategy Pattern        │\n    39→│  ▼                       │                   ▼                         │\n    40→│ ┌──────────┐    emits ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐             │\n    41→│ │ DriftMon │──────────▶  JobScheduler│────▶ ModelRegistry│             │\n    42→│ └──────────┘          └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘             │\n    43→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n    44→```\n    45→\n    46→Legend:\n    47→\n    48→* **Controller** – Pipeline orchestrator (`controller/*.c`).\n    49→* **Model** – NLP/ML pipeline (`model/*.c`), pluggable via Strategy Pattern.\n    50→* **View** – Dashboards & WebSocket push (`view/*.c`).\n    51→* **Feature Store** – External component accessed via gRPC (`external/featstore`).\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→## 3  Directory Layout\n    56→\n    57→```text\n    58→lexilearn_orchestrator/\n    59→├── include/\n    60→│   ├── lexilearn.h         # Umbrella public header\n    61→│   ├── controller.h        # Controller API\n    62→│   ├── model.h             # Model Strategy API\n    63→│   └── view.h              # Visualization API\n    64→├── src/\n    65→│   ├── controller/\n    66→│   ├── model/\n    67→│   ├── view/\n    68→│   ├── util/\n    69→│   └── main.c              # Embeddable entry\n    70→├── conf/\n    71→│   └── orchestrator.toml   # Build-time & run-time parameters\n    72→└── docs/\n    73→    └── architecture.md\n    74→```\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## 4  Key Design Patterns\n    79→\n    80→| Concern              | Pattern            | Implementation Notes                                     |\n    81→|----------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|\n    82→| Pipeline management  | Pipeline Pattern   | `controller/pipeline.c` composes discrete stages.        |\n    83→| Model hot-swapping   | Strategy Pattern   | `model/strategy_*.c` registered in a function pointer v-table. |\n    84→| Training job factory | Factory Pattern    | `controller/job_factory.c` builds diverse training jobs. |\n    85→| Experiment tracking  | Model Registry     | `model/registry.c` (backed by SQLite + JSONB).           |\n    86→| Drift detection       | Observer Pattern  | `util/drift_monitor.c` emits `EVENT_DRIFT_*`.            |\n    87→\n    88→---\n    89→\n    90→## 5  Runtime Flow\n    91→\n    92→```mermaid\n    93→sequenceDiagram\n    94→    participant LMS\n    95→    participant Controller\n    96→    participant Model\n    97→    participant Registry\n    98→    participant View\n    99→\n   100→    LMS->>Controller: ingest_snapshot()\n   101→    Controller->>Model: preprocess()\n   102→    Model->>Model: train() / predict()\n   103→    Model-->>Controller: metrics, model_id\n   104→    Controller->>Registry: log_experiment()\n   105→    alt Drift detected\n   106→        Controller->>Controller: schedule_retraining()\n   107→    end\n   108→    Controller-->>View: push_metrics_ws()\n   109→```\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## 6  Concurrency & Memory Safety\n   114→\n   115→1. **Actor Model Threads**  \n   116→   Controller spins lightweight actors (`pthread`) pinned to CPU cores:\n   117→   * `actor_ingest`\n   118→   * `actor_train`\n   119→   * `actor_drift`\n   120→   * `actor_view`\n   121→\n   122→2. **Zero-Copy Buffers**  \n   123→   Multimodal records reside in lock-free, ring buffers (`util/ringbuf.c`).\n   124→\n   125→3. **Ownership Annotation**  \n   126→   Every public API symbol suffixed with `_ref` uses **Reference-Counted Handles**.\n   127→\n   128→Example:\n   129→\n   130→```c\n   131→// src/model/model.c\n   132→ml_model_ref\n   133→ml_model_create(const char *strategy_name, const ml_hparam_set *hparams) {\n   134→    ml_model_impl *impl = strategy_vtable_get(strategy_name);\n   135→    if (!impl) { return NULL; }\n   136→    ml_model_ref ref = RC_ALLOC(sizeof(struct ml_model));\n   137→    ref->impl = impl;\n   138→    ref->impl->init(ref, hparams);\n   139→    return ref;\n   140→}\n   141→```\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→## 7  Error Handling Philosophy\n   146→\n   147→* **Fail-Fast** in development, **graceful degradation** in production.\n   148→* Rich machine-parsable error structs:\n   149→\n   150→```c\n   151→typedef struct {\n   152→    ll_err_code code;        /* Enumerated error code */\n   153→    char        msg[256];    /* Human-readable context */\n   154→    char        trace_id[64];/* Correlation ID for logs & metrics */\n   155→} ll_error_t;\n   156→```\n   157→\n   158→* Controller propagates root-cause via Observer Pattern (`EVENT_ERROR`),\n   159→  surfaced as HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n   160→\n   161→---\n   162→\n   163→## 8  Extending the Model Layer\n   164→\n   165→To add a custom summarizer:\n   166→\n   167→1. Implement `model/strategy_summarizer.c`, exporting:\n   168→   ```c\n   169→   const ml_strategy_vtable *lexi_summarizer_strategy(void);\n   170→   ```\n   171→2. Register the strategy in `model/strategy_registry.c`:\n   172→   ```c\n   173→   REGISTER_STRATEGY(\"lexi_summarizer\", lexi_summarizer_strategy);\n   174→   ```\n   175→3. LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator – System Architecture  \n_Author: Core Platform Team_  \n_Last updated: 2024-05-30_\n\n---\n\n## 1  Overview\n\nLexiLearn MVC Orchestrator (repo root: `ml_nlp/lexilearn_orchestrator`) is a **modular, C-based, MVC platform** for large-scale, adaptive language-learning applications.  \nThe orchestrator stitches together Machine-Learning Ops essentials—hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, automated retraining—behind a clean Controller façade exposing a single public header `lexilearn.h`.\n\nKey design drivers:\n\n* **High throughput** ingestion of multimodal classroom data.\n* **Pluggable NLP/ML strategies** switchable at run-time.\n* **Deterministic** builds, reproducible experiments, and strong type-safety.\n* **Embeddability** in web servers, desktop LMS plug-ins, and edge devices.\n\n---\n\n## 2  Top-Level Component Diagram\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                            Application Layer                           │\n│ ┌────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐                 │\n│ │  REST API  │    │ CLI Pipeline │    │  Batch Jobs  │ <─┐             │\n│ └────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘   │ Application │\n└──────│───────────────────│───────────────────│───────────┘             │\n       ▼                   ▼                   ▼                         ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                           MVC Orchestrator                             │\n│ ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐                   │\n│ │  View    │◀──────▶│ Controller│◀──────▶│  Model   │                   │\n│ └──────────┘        └──────────┘        └──────────┘                   │\n│  │                       ▲                   │                         │\n│  │ Observer Pattern      │ Factory Pattern   │ Strategy Pattern        │\n│  ▼                       │                   ▼                         │\n│ ┌──────────┐    emits ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐             │\n│ │ DriftMon │──────────▶  JobScheduler│────▶ ModelRegistry│             │\n│ └──────────┘          └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nLegend:\n\n* **Controller** – Pipeline orchestrator (`controller/*.c`).\n* **Model** – NLP/ML pipeline (`model/*.c`), pluggable via Strategy Pattern.\n* **View** – Dashboards & WebSocket push (`view/*.c`).\n* **Feature Store** – External component accessed via gRPC (`external/featstore`).\n\n---\n\n## 3  Directory Layout\n\n```text\nlexilearn_orchestrator/\n├── include/\n│   ├── lexilearn.h         # Umbrella public header\n│   ├── controller.h        # Controller API\n│   ├── model.h             # Model Strategy API\n│   └── view.h              # Visualization API\n├── src/\n│   ├── controller/\n│   ├── model/\n│   ├── view/\n│   ├── util/\n│   └── main.c              # Embeddable entry\n├── conf/\n│   └── orchestrator.toml   # Build-time & run-time parameters\n└── docs/\n    └── architecture.md\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4  Key Design Patterns\n\n| Concern              | Pattern            | Implementation Notes                                     |\n|----------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|\n| Pipeline management  | Pipeline Pattern   | `controller/pipeline.c` composes discrete stages.        |\n| Model hot-swapping   | Strategy Pattern   | `model/strategy_*.c` registered in a function pointer v-table. |\n| Training job factory | Factory Pattern    | `controller/job_factory.c` builds diverse training jobs. |\n| Experiment tracking  | Model Registry     | `model/registry.c` (backed by SQLite + JSONB).           |\n| Drift detection       | Observer Pattern  | `util/drift_monitor.c` emits `EVENT_DRIFT_*`.            |\n\n---\n\n## 5  Runtime Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant LMS\n    participant Controller\n    participant Model\n    participant Registry\n    participant View\n\n    LMS->>Controller: ingest_snapshot()\n    Controller->>Model: preprocess()\n    Model->>Model: train() / predict()\n    Model-->>Controller: metrics, model_id\n    Controller->>Registry: log_experiment()\n    alt Drift detected\n        Controller->>Controller: schedule_retraining()\n    end\n    Controller-->>View: push_metrics_ws()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6  Concurrency & Memory Safety\n\n1. **Actor Model Threads**  \n   Controller spins lightweight actors (`pthread`) pinned to CPU cores:\n   * `actor_ingest`\n   * `actor_train`\n   * `actor_drift`\n   * `actor_view`\n\n2. **Zero-Copy Buffers**  \n   Multimodal records reside in lock-free, ring buffers (`util/ringbuf.c`).\n\n3. **Ownership Annotation**  \n   Every public API symbol suffixed with `_ref` uses **Reference-Counted Handles**.\n\nExample:\n\n```c\n// src/model/model.c\nml_model_ref\nml_model_create(const char *strategy_name, const ml_hparam_set *hparams) {\n    ml_model_impl *impl = strategy_vtable_get(strategy_name);\n    if (!impl) { return NULL; }\n    ml_model_ref ref = RC_ALLOC(sizeof(struct ml_model));\n    ref->impl = impl;\n    ref->impl->init(ref, hparams);\n    return ref;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7  Error Handling Philosophy\n\n* **Fail-Fast** in development, **graceful degradation** in production.\n* Rich machine-parsable error structs:\n\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    ll_err_code code;        /* Enumerated error code */\n    char        msg[256];    /* Human-readable context */\n    char        trace_id[64];/* Correlation ID for logs & metrics */\n} ll_error_t;\n```\n\n* Controller propagates root-cause via Observer Pattern (`EVENT_ERROR`),\n  surfaced as HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n\n---\n\n## 8  Extending the Model Layer\n\nTo add a custom summarizer:\n\n1. Implement `model/strategy_summarizer.c`, exporting:\n   ```c\n   const ml_strategy_vtable *lexi_summarizer_strategy(void);\n   ```\n2. Register the strategy in `model/strategy_registry.c`:\n   ```c\n   REGISTER_STRATEGY(\"lexi_summarizer\", lexi_summarizer_strategy);\n   ```\n3. Specify in `orchestrator.toml`:\n   ```toml\n   [strategy]\n   active = \"lexi_summarizer\"\n   ```\nStrategy picked up at run-time—no Controller code touched.\n\n---\n\n## 9  Build & Deployment\n\n```sh\n$ mkdir build && cd build\n$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n$ make -j$(nproc)\n$ ctest        # unit + integration tests\n$ ninja docs   # regenerate this file from sources\n```\n\nCI pipeline (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) enforces:\n\n* Clang-Tidy, MISRA-C subset.\n* Code coverage ≥ 85 %.\n* Static analysis (`cppcheck`, `infer`).\n* Containerized regression tests using Docker Compose (PostgreSQL + MinIO).\n\n---\n\n## 10  MLOps Integration\n\n* **Hyper-Parameter Sweep** – `controller/hparam_tuner.c` interfaces with Optuna via gRPC.\n* **Model Registry** – Lightweight SQLite + S3 artifacts, versioned by semantic hash.\n* **Drift Monitoring** – KL divergence threshold triggers retraining jobs.\n* **Automated Retraining** – Controller pushes jobs onto RabbitMQ queue; workers auto-scale via K8s HPA.\n* **Audit Trail** – `model/registry.c` persists lineage: dataset → commit SHA → metrics.\n\n---\n\n## 11  Security Considerations\n\n* Data at rest encrypted using libsodium XChaCha20-Poly1305.\n* TLS 1.3 enforced for gRPC and WebSocket endpoints.\n* Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) integrated with LMS OAuth2 scopes.\n* Secrets managed via HashiCorp Vault sidecar injector.\n\n---\n\n## 12  Future Roadmap\n\n| Version | Planned Feature                               | Status |\n|---------|-----------------------------------------------|--------|\n| v2.1    | Federated Learning across districts           | 🟡 PoC |\n| v2.2    | On-device speech assessment (Edge TPU)        | ⬜ Backlog |\n| v3.0    | Rust FFI for safety-critical routines         | ⬜ Backlog |\n\n---\n\n## 13  Glossary\n\n* **Feature Store** – Centralized service storing engineered features for offline & online use.\n* **Model Registry** – Database tracking model versions, metrics, and provenance.\n* **Strategy Pattern** – Encapsulates interchangeable algorithms behind a stable API.\n* **Observer Pattern** – Event dispatcher decoupling producers & consumers.\n\n---\n\n© 2024 LexiLearn Inc. – Licensed under the Apache 2.0 License\n```","numLines":242,"startLine":1,"totalLines":242}}}

  observer_t   *subscriber)\n   169→{\n   170→    if (!observable || !subscriber || !subscriber->on_event)\n   171→        return -1; /* invalid args */\n   172→\n   173→    /* Prevent duplicate p;\n   214→}\n   215→\n   216→/* ====  Metric Implementations  =========================================== */\n   217→static double\n   218→lexi_compute_psi(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n   219→{\n   220→    double psi = 0.0;\n   221→    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n   222→    {\n   223→        const double e = ref[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : ref[i];\n   224→        const double a = act[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : act[i];\n   225→        psi += (a - e) * log(a / e);\n   226→    }\n   227→    return psi;\n   228→}\n   229→\n   230→static double\n   231→lexi_compute_kl(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n   232→{\n   233→    double kl = 0.0;\n   234→    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n   235→    {\n   236→        double a = act[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : act[i];\n   237→        double r = ref[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : ref[i];\n   238→        kl += a * log(a / r);\n   239→    }\n   240→    return kl;\n   241→}\n   242→\n   243→static double\n   244→lexi_compute_wasserstein(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n   245→{\n   246→    /* 1-dimensional 1-Wasserstein distance for histograms */\n   247→    double cum_ref = 0.0, cum_act = 0.0, wass = 0.0;\n   248→    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n   249→    {\n   250→        cum_ref += ref[i];\n   251→        cum_act += act[i];\n   252→        wass += fabs(cum_ref - cum_act);\n Simply include\n *  this file in a single translation unit with\n *      #define LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION\n *  before the include directive to generate the implementation.\n *\n *  The API is thread-safe and uses an Observer pattern so that external\n *  subsystems (e.g., the Retraining Scheduler) can subscribe to drift events.\n * =============================================================================\n */\n\n#ifndef LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_H\n#define LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_H\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* ====  Standard Library Dependencies  ===================================== */\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* ====  Versioning  ========================================================= */\n#define LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_VERSION_MAJOR 1\n#define LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_VERSION_MINOR 0\n#define LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_VERSION_PATCH 0\n\n/* ====  Compile-time Configuration  ======================================== */\n#ifndef LEXI_MAX_BINS\n#   define LEXI_MAX_BINS 32          /* Upper bound on histogram bins */\n#endif\n\n#ifndef LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS\n#   define LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS 16     /* Max Observer callbacks per detector */\n#endif\n\n#ifndef LEXI_MODEL_ID_LEN\n#   define LEXI_MODEL_ID_LEN 64      /* Length of model identifier string */\n#endif\n\n/* ====  Error Codes  ======================================================== */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    LEXI_OK                = 0,\n    LEXI_EINVAL            = 1,  /* Invalid argument                     */\n    LEXI_EOOR              = 2,  /* Out of range                         */\n    LEXI_ENOMEM            = 3,  /* Allocation failure                   */\n    LEXI_ETHREAD           = 4,  /* Threading / lock failure             */\n    LEXI_ELISTENER_FULL    = 5,  /* Listener array is already saturated  */\n    LEXI_EUNKNOWN          = 255 /* Unknown / unspecified error          */\n} lexi_error_t;\n\n/* ====  Public API Types  =================================================== */\n\n/*  Drift score computation strategy.  */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    LEXI_DRIFT_PSI      = 0,    /* Population Stability Index           */\n    LEXI_DRIFT_KL       = 1,    /* Kullback-Leibler divergence          */\n    LEXI_DRIFT_WASSER   = 2,    /* 1-Wasserstein (a.k.a Earth-Mover)    */\n} score          */\n    double    threshold;       /* User-defined threshold that was crossed */\n    time_t    timestamp;       /* UTC epoch-seconds of detection          */\n} lexi_drift_event_t;\n\n/*  Function signature for Observer callback.  */\ntypedef void (*lexi_drift_listener_t)(const lexi_drift_event_t* event,\n                                      void*                     user_ctx);\n\n/*  Opaque drift detector handle.  */\ntypedef struct lexi_drift_detector lexi_drift_detector_t;\n\n/*  Configuration object supplied at construction time.  */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char                 model_id[LEXI_MODEL_ID_LEN];\n    size_t               num_bins;           /* Must be ≤ LEXI_MAX_BINS     */\n    double               threshold;          /*  : User-provided configuration object.\n *      err   : Optional pointer to receive an error code.\n *\n *  Returns\n *  -------\n *      Non-NULL pointer on success; NULL on failure.\n */\nlexi_drift_detector_t*\nlexi_drift_create(const lexi_drift_cfg_t* cfg, lexi_error_t* err);\n\n/*\n *  Destroy a detector and free all associated resources.\n */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_destroy(lexi_drift_detector_t* detector);\n\n/*\n *  Feed a batch of observed probabilities into the detector.\n *\n *  Arguments\n *  ---------\n *      detector : Handle *      err      : Optional out-parameter for error status.\n *\n *  Notes\n *  -----\n *      Users are expected to pre-bucketize raw feature values into the same\n *      bin boundaries as the reference distribution used during model\n *      training.  All values in `probs` must be non-negative and sum to 1.0.\n */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_update(lexi_drift_detector_t* detector,\n                  const double*          probs,\n                  lexi_error_t*          err);\n\n/*\n *  Query whether drift has already been observed (non-destructive).\n *\n *  Returns\n *  -------\n *      true  : Drift condition currently active.\n *      false : No drift identified.\n */\nbool\nlexi_drift_is_drifting(const lexi_drift_detector_t* detector,\n                       double*                      out_score /* optional */);\n\n/*\n *  Attach an Observer callback.\n *\n *  The callback is invoked asynchronously *within the context of\n *  lexi_drift_update()* immediately after drift is detected. To prevent\n *  deadlocks, callbacks must not call back into the detector API.\n */\nlexi_error_t\nlexi_drift_add_listener(lexi_drift_detector_t* detector,\n                        lexi_drift_listener_t  fn,\n                        void*                  user_ctx);\n\n/*\n *  Remove an Observer callback. (a) : (b))\n#define LEXI_MAX(a,b) ((a) > (b) ? (a) : (b))\n\nstruct lexi_drift_detector\n{\n    lexi_drift_cfg_t    cfg;\n    size_t              sample_count;                  /* # observations      */\n    double              reference[LEXI_MAX_BINS];      /* Training dist.      */\n    double              rolling_actual[LEXI_MAX_BINS]; /* Sliding window dist */\n    double              last_score;\n    bool                drifting;\n\n    /* Observer Pattern */\n    lexi_drift_listener_t listeners[LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS];\n    void*                 listener_ctx[LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS];\n    size_t                listener_cnt;\n\n    /* Thread-safety */\n    pthread_mutex_t      mtx;\n};\n\n/* ====  Utility: Safe allocation wrapper  ================================== */\nstatic inline void* lexi_xcalloc(size_t n, size_t sz)\n{\n    void* p = calloc(n, sz);\n    return p;\n}\n\n/* ====  Metric Implementations  =========================================== */\nstatic double\nlexi_compute_psi(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n{\n    double psi = 0.0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        const double e = ref[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : ref[i];\n        const double a = act[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : act[i];\n        psi += (a - e) * log(a / e);\n    }\n    return psi;\n}\n\nstatic double\nlexi_compute_kl(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n{\n    double kl = 0.0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        double a = act[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : act[i];\n        double r = ref[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : ref[i];\n        kl += a * log(a / r);\n    }\n    return kl;\n}\n\nstatic double\nlexi_compute_wasserstein(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n{\n    /* 1-dimensional 1-Wasserstein distance for histograms */\n    double cum_ref = 0.0, cum_act = 0.0, wass = 0.0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        cum_ref += ref[i];\n        cum_act += act[i];\n        wass += fabs(cum_ref - cum_act);\n    }\n    return wass;\n}\n\n/* ====  Private helper: notify listeners  ================================== */\nstatic void\nlexi_notify(const lexi_drift_detector_t* det)\n{\n    lexi_drift_event_t evt;\n    strncpy(evt.model_id, det->cfg.model_id, LEXI_MODEL_ID_LEN);\n    evt.score     = det->last_score;\n    evt.threshold = det->cfg.threshold;\n    evt.timestamp = time(NULL);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < det->listener_cnt; ++i)\n    {\n        det->listeners[i](&evt, det->listener_ctx[i]);\n    }\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: Create  ================================================= */\nlexi_drift_detector_t*\nlexi_drift_create(const lexi_drift_cfg_t* cfg, lexi_error_t* err)\n{\n    if (!cfg || cfg->num_bins == 0 || cfg->num_bins > LEXI_MAX_BINS)\n    {\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_EINVAL;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    lexi_drift_detector_t* det = (lexi_drift_detector_t*)lexi_xcalloc(1, sizeof(*det));\n    if (!det)\n    {\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_ENOMEM;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    det->cfg = *cfg;\n    /* Initialize reference distribution to uniform until calibrated */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < cfg->num_bins; ++i)\n        det->reference[i] = 1.0 / (double)cfg->num_bins;\n\n    if (pthread_mutex_init(&det->mtx, NULL) != 0)\n    {\n        free(det);\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_ETHREAD;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    if (err) *err = LEXI_OK;\n    return det;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: Destroy  ============================================== */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_destroy(lexi_drift_detector_t* det)\n{\n    if (!det) return;\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&det->mtx);\n    free(det);\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: Update  =============================================== */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_update(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                  const double*          probs,\n                  lexi_error_t*          err)\n{\n    if (!det || !probs)\n    {\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_EINVAL;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n    const size_t k = det->cfg.num_bins;\n\n    /* Exponential moving average for rolling distribution */\n    const double alpha = 1.0 / (double)LEXI_MAX(1, det->cfg.window);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        double p = probs[i];\n        if (p < 0.0)\n        {\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n            if (err) *err = LEXI_EINVAL;\n            return;\n        }\n        det->rolling_actual[i] =\n            (1.0 - alpha) * det->rolling_actual[i] + alpha * p;\n    }\n\n    /* Compute drift metric */\n    switch (det->cfg.metric)\n    {\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_PSI:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_psi(det->reference,\n                                               det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_KL:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_kl(det->reference,\n                                              det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_WASSER:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_wasserstein(det->reference,\n                                                       det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        default:\n            det->last_score = 0.0;\n            break;\n    }\n\n    bool drift_now = det->last_score >= det->cfg.threshold;\n    if (drift_now && !det->drifting)\n    {\n        det->drifting = true;\n        lexi_notify(det); /* fire Observer callbacks */\n    }\n    else if (!drift_now && det->drifting)\n    {\n        det->drifting = false; /* reset flag */\n    }\n\n    det->sample_count++;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n\n    if (err) *err = LEXI_OK;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: is_drifting  ========================================== */\nbool\nlexi_drift_is_drifting(const lexi_drift_detector_t* det, double* out_score)\n{\n    if (!det) return false;\n    if (out_score) *out_score = det->last_score;\n    return det->drifting;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: add/remove listener  =================================== */\nlexi_error_t\nlexi_drift_add_listener(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                        lexi_drift_listener_t  fn,\n                        void*                  ctx)\n{\n    if (!det || !fn) return LEXI_EINVAL;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n\n    if (det->listener_cnt >= LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n        return LEXI_ELISTENER_FULL;\n    }\n    det->listeners[det->listener_cnt]     = fn;\n    det->listener_ctx[det->listener_cnt]  = ctx;\n    det->listener_cnt++;\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n    return LEXI_OK;\n}\n\nlexi_error_t\nlexi_drift_remove_listener(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                           lexi_drift_listener_t  fn,\n                           void*                  ctx)\n{\n    if (!det || !fn) return LEXI_EINVAL;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n    size_t i = 0;\n    while (i < det->listener_cnt)\n    {\n        if (det->listeners[i] == fn && det->listener_ctx[i] == ctx)\n        {\n            /* shift elements */\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < det->listener_cnt; ++j)\n            {\n                det->listeners[j - 1]    = det->listeners[j];\n                det->listener_ctx[j - 1] = det->listener_ctx[j];\n            }\n            det->listener_cnt--;\n            /* do not increment i to catch duplicate matches */\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            ++i;\n        }\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n    return LEXI_OK;\n}\n\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION */\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_H */\n","numLines":445,"startLine":1,"totalLines":445}}}

*  lexilearn_orchestrator/src/controller/monitoring/model_monitor.h\n *\n *  Copyright (c) 2024 LexiLearn\n *\n *  Production-grade interface for Controller-level Model Monitoring.\n *  -----------------------------------------------------------------\n *  The Model Monitor is responsible for:\n *    • Persistently tracking model-level metrics (latency, accuracy, drift, etc.)\n *    • Emitting Observer-pattern events to trigger automated retraining\n *    • Acting as an abstraction layer so that multiple drift-detection\n *      strategies can be swapped in without touching Versioning                                  */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_MAJOR  1\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_MINOR  0\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_PATCH  0\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Error Codes                                 */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    MM_OK = 0,                  /* Success */\n    MM_ERR_INVALID_ARG = -1,    /* Invalid function argument */\n    MM_ERR_MEM_ALLOC   = -2,    /* Memory allocation failure */\n    MM_ERR_IO          = -3,    /* Persistent-store I/O failure */\n    MM_ERR_STATE       = -4,    /* Monitor is in invalid state */\n    MM_ERR_INTERNAL    = -5,    /* Catch-all for unexpected errors */\n} mm_status_t;\n\n/* Safe to call from within the\n * callback itself (will take effect after callback returns).\n */\nmm_status_t mm_remove_observer(mm_model_handle_t *handle,\n                               mm_observer_fn     fn,\n                               void              *user_data);\n\n/**\n * mm_register_drift_detector()\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Plug-in a new drift detection strategy. 77→    RT_METRIC_F1,\n    78→    RT_METRIC_CUSTOM\n    79→} rt_metric_t;\n    80→\n    81→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    82→ *  Callback Signature\n    83→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n    84→/*\n    85→ *  The orchestrator registers exactly one callback that gets invoked\n    86→ *  whenever drift (a) : (b))\n   144→#define RT_MAX(a,b) ((a) > (b) ? (a) : (b))\n   145→\n   146→/* Return current time in milliseconds since epoch – for internal bookkeeping\n   147→ * when caller passes 0 for timestamp_ms.\n   148→ */\n   149→static uint64_t _rt_now_ms(void)\n   150→{\n   151→    struct timespec ts;\n   152→    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n   153→    return ((uint64_t) ts.tv_sec) * 1000ULL + (uint64_t) (ts.tv_nsec / 1000000ULL);\n   154→}\n   155→\n   156→/* Metric ring-buffer element */\n   157→typedef struct\n   158→{\n   159→    double   value;\n   160→    uint64_t ts_ms;\n   161→} rt_sample_t;\n   162→\n   163→/* Model registry entry */\n   164→typedef struct\n   165→{\n   166→    bool             in_use;\n   167→    char             model_id[RT_MODEL_ID_MAX];\n   168→\n   169→    /* configuration */\n   170→    rt_metric_t      metric_type;\n   171→    double           drift_threshold_pct;                 /* current number of samples */\n   177→    bool             retrain_pending;       /* drift detected, waiting for handler */\n   178→} rt_model_entry_t;\n   179→\n   180→/* Global registry */\n   181→static struct\n   182→{\n   183→    pthread_mutex_t      mtx;\n   184→    rt_model_entry_t     entries[RT_MAX_MODELS];\n   185→    rt_retrain_callback_t callback;\n   186→    bool                 initialized;\n   187→} _rt_state = {\n   188→    .mtx          = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER,\n   189→    .entries      = { {0} },\n   190→    .callback     = NULL,\n   191→    .initialized  = false\n   192→};\n   193→\n   194→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   195→ *  Internal Functions\n   196→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   197→\n   198→/* Thread-safe lookup (caller must hold lock) */\n   199→static rt_model_entry_t *_rt_find_entry(const char *model_id)\n   200→{\n   201→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   202→    {\n   203→        if (_rt_state.entries[i].in_use &&\n   204→            strncmp(_rt_state.entries[i].model_id, model_id, RT_MODEL_ID_MAX) == 0)\n   205→        {\n   206→            return &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   207→        }\n   208→    }\n   209→    return NULL;\n   210→}\n   211→\n   212→/* Compute average of N most recent samples from ring buffer */\n   213→static double _rt_avg_last_n(const rt_model_entry_t *ent, size_t n)\n   214→{\n   215→    if (ent->count == 0) { return 0.0; }\n   216→    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n   217→    double sum = 0.0;\n   218→    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n   219→    {\n   220→        size_t idx = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - 1 - i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   221→        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n   222→    }\n   223→    return sum / (double) n;\n   224→}\n   225→\n   226→/* Compute baseline average consisting of the oldest N samples */\n   227→static double _rt_avg_first_n(const rt_model_entry_t *ent, size_t n)\n   228→{\n   229→    if (ent->count == 0) { return 0.0; }\n   230→    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n   231→    double sum = 0.0;\n   232→    size_t start = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - ent->count) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   233→    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n   234→    {\n   235→        size_t idx = (start + i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   236→        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n   237→    }\n   238→    return sum / (double) n;\n   239→}\n   240→\n   241→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   242→ *  Public API – Implementation\n   243→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   244→rt_status_t rt_trigger_init(rt_retrain_callback_t cb)\n   245→{\n   246→    if (cb == NULL) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   247→\n   248→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   249→    _rt_state.callback    = cb;\n   250→    _rt_state.initialized = true;\n   251→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   252→\n   253→    return RT_OK;\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→void rt_trigger_shutdown(void)\n   257→{\n   258→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   259→    memset(_rt_state.entries, 0, sizeof(_rt_state.entries));\n   260→    _rt_state.callback    = NULL;\n   261→    _rt_state.initialized = false;\n   262→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   263→}\n   264→\n   265→rt_status_t rt_register_model(const char *model_id,\n   266→                              rt_metric_t metric,\n   267→                              double drift_threshold_pct)\n   268→{\n   269→    if (!model_id || drift_threshold_pct <= 0.0) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   270→\n   271→    rt_status_t status = RT_ERR_FULL;\n   272→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   273→\n   274→    /* duplicate? */\n   275→    if (_rt_find_entry(model_id) != NULL)\n   276→    {\n   277→        status = RT_ERR_ALREADY_EXISTS;\n   278→        goto out;\n   279→    }\n   280→\n   281→    /* locate free slot */\n   282→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   283→    {\n   284→        if (!_rt_state.entries[i].in_use)\n   285→        {\n   286→            rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   287→            memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n   288→            ent->in_use              = true;\n   289→            ent->metric_type         = metric;\n   290→            ent->drift_threshold_pct = drift_threshold_pct;\n   291→            strncpy(ent->model_id, model_id, RT_MODEL_ID_MAX - 1);\n   292→            status = RT_OK;\n   293→            goto out;\n   294→        }\n   295→    }\n   296→\n   297→out:\n   298→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   299→    return status;\n   300→}\n   301→\n   302→rt_status_t rt_unregister_model(const char *model_id)\n   303→{\n   304→    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   305→\n   306→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   307→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   308→    if (ent)\n   309→    {\n   310→        memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n   311→        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   312→        return RT_OK;\n   313→    }\n   314→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   315→    return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n   316→}\n   317→\n   318→rt_status_t rt_record_metric(const char *model_id,\n   319→                             double      value,\n   320→                             uint64_t    timestamp_ms)\n   321→{\n   322→    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   323→    if (timestamp_ms == 0) { timestamp_ms = _rt_now_ms(); }\n   324→\n   325→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   326→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   327→    if (!ent)\n   328→    {\n   329→        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   330→        return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n   331→    }\n   332→\n   333→    /* insert into ring buffer */\n   334→    ent->samples[ent->head].value  = value;\n   335→    ent->samples[ent->head].ts_ms  = timestamp_ms;\n   336→    ent->head   = (ent->head + 1) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   337→    ent->count  = RT_MIN(ent->count + 1, RT_MAX_SAMPLES);\n   338→\n   339→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   340→    return RT_OK;\n   341→}\n   342→\n   343→/* Determine drift for a single entry (lock must be held) */\n   344→static bool _rt_drift_detected(rt_model_entry_t *ent)\n   345→{\n   346→    if (ent->count < RT_BASELINE_WINDOW * 2) { return false; }\n   347→\n   348→    double baseline = _rt_avg_first_n(ent, RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n   349→    double current  = _rt_avg_last_n(ent,  RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n   350→\n   351→    if (baseline == 0.0) { return false; } /* avoid div-by-zero */\n   352→\n   353→    double delta_pct;\n   354→    if (ent->metric_type == RT_METRIC_LOSS)\n   355→    {\n   356→        /* increase in loss indicates drift */\n   357→        delta_pct = ((current - baseline) / baseline) * 100.0;\n   358→        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n   359→    }\n   360→    else\n   361→    {\n   362→        /* decrease in metric (accuracy, F1, etc.) indicates drift */\n   363→        delta_pct = ((baseline - current) / baseline) * 100.0;\n   364→        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n   365→    }\n   366→}\n   367→\n   368→void rt_check_and_schedule(void)\n   369→{\n   370→    if (!_rt_state.initialized || !_rt_state.callback) { return; }\n   371→\n   372→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   373→\n   374→    /* scan registry */\n   375→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   376→    {\n   377→        rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   378→        if (!ent->in_use || ent->retrain_pending) { continue; }\n   379→\n   380→        if (_rt_drift_detected(ent))\n   381→        {\n   382→            ent->retrain_pending = true;\n   383→            /* Unlock during callback to avoid deadlocks */\n   384→            pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   385→            bool ok = _rt_state.callback(ent->model_id);\n   386→            pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   387→\n   388→            if (ok)\n   389→            {\n   390→                /* Callback accepted the scheduling – reset samples to establish new baseline */\n   391→                ent->count            = 0;\n   392→                ent->head             = 0;\n   393→                ent->retrain_pending  = false;\n   394→            }\n   395→            /* else: keep pending flag set for future retries */\n   396→        }\n   397→    }\n   398→\n   399→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   400→}\n   401→\n   402→bool rt_has_pending_retraining(const char *model_id)\n   403→{\n   404→    if (!model_id) { return false; }\n   405→    bool pending = false;\n   406→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   407→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   408→    if (ent) { pending = ent->retrain_pending; }\n   409→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   410→    return pending;\n   411→}\n   412→\n   413→#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_IMPLEMENTATION */\n   414→#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_H */\n   415→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. }\n    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n    {\n        size_t idx = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - 1 - i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n    }\n    return sum / (double) n;\n}\n\n/* Compute baseline average consisting of the oldest N samples */\nstatic double _rt_avg_first_n(const rt_model_entry_t *ent, size_t n)\n{\n    if (ent->count == 0) { return 0.0; }\n    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    size_t start = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - ent->count) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n    {\n        size_t idx = (start + i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n    }\n    return sum / (double) n;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Public API – Implementation\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nrt_status_t rt_trigger_init(rt_retrain_callback_t cb)\n{\n    if (cb == NULL) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    _rt_state.callback    = cb;\n    _rt_state.initialized = true;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n\n    return RT_OK;\n}\n\nvoid rt_trigger_shutdown(void)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    memset(_rt_state.entries, 0, sizeof(_rt_state.entries));\n    _rt_state.callback    = NULL;\n    _rt_state.initialized = false;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n}\n\nrt_status_t rt_register_model(const char *model_id,\n                              rt_metric_t metric,\n                              double drift_threshold_pct)\n{\n    if (!model_id || drift_threshold_pct <= 0.0) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n\n    rt_status_t status = RT_ERR_FULL;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n\n    /* duplicate? */\n    if (_rt_find_entry(model_id) != NULL)\n    {\n        status = RT_ERR_ALREADY_EXISTS;\n        goto out;\n    }\n\n    /* locate free slot */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n    {\n        if (!_rt_state.entries[i].in_use)\n        {\n            rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n            memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n            ent->in_use              = true;\n            ent->metric_type         = metric;\n            ent->drift_threshold_pct = drift_threshold_pct;\n            strncpy(ent->model_id, model_id, RT_MODEL_ID_MAX - 1);\n            status = RT_OK;\n            goto out;\n        }\n    }\n\nout:\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    return status;\n}\n\nrt_status_t rt_unregister_model(const char *model_id)\n{\n    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n    if (ent)\n    {\n        memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n        return RT_OK;\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n}\n\nrt_status_t rt_record_metric(const char *model_id,\n                             double      value,\n                             uint64_t    timestamp_ms)\n{\n    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n    if (timestamp_ms == 0) { timestamp_ms = _rt_now_ms(); }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n    if (!ent)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n        return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n    }\n\n    /* insert into ring buffer */\n    ent->samples[ent->head].value  = value;\n    ent->samples[ent->head].ts_ms  = timestamp_ms;\n    ent->head   = (ent->head + 1) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n    ent->count  = RT_MIN(ent->count + 1, RT_MAX_SAMPLES);\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    return RT_OK;\n}\n\n/* Determine drift for a single entry (lock must be held) */\nstatic bool _rt_drift_detected(rt_model_entry_t *ent)\n{\n    if (ent->count < RT_BASELINE_WINDOW * 2) { return false; }\n\n    double baseline = _rt_avg_first_n(ent, RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n    double current  = _rt_avg_last_n(ent,  RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n\n    if (baseline == 0.0) { return false; } /* avoid div-by-zero */\n\n    double delta_pct;\n    if (ent->metric_type == RT_METRIC_LOSS)\n    {\n        /* increase in loss indicates drift */\n        delta_pct = ((current - baseline) / baseline) * 100.0;\n        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        /* decrease in metric (accuracy, F1, etc.) indicates drift */\n        delta_pct = ((baseline - current) / baseline) * 100.0;\n        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid rt_check_and_schedule(void)\n{\n    if (!_rt_state.initialized || !_rt_state.callback) { return; }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n\n    /* scan registry */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n    {\n        rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n        if (!ent->in_use || ent->retrain_pending) { continue; }\n\n        if (_rt_drift_detected(ent))\n        {\n            ent->retrain_pending = true;\n            /* Unlock during callback to avoid deadlocks */\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n            bool ok = _rt_state.callback(ent->model_id);\n            pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n\n            if (ok)\n            {\n                /* Callback accepted the scheduling – reset samples to establish new baseline */\n                ent->count            = 0;\n                ent->head             = 0;\n                ent->retrain_pending  = false;\n            }\n            /* else: keep pending flag set for future retries */\n        }\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n}\n\nbool rt_has_pending_retraining(const char *model_id)\n{\n    if (!model_id) { return false; }\n    bool pending = false;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n    if (ent) { pending = ent->retrain_pending; }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n    return pending;\n}\n\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_IMPLEMENTATION */\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_H */\n","numLines":415,"startLine":1,"totalLines":415}}}

------------------------------------------------ */\n    pmgr->ingestor = data_ingestor_factory_create(ingestor_cfg);\n    if (!pmgr->ingestor) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to instantiate DataIngestor\");\n        free(pmgr);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    pmgr->job_factory    = job_factory;\n    pmgr->registry       = registry;\n    pmgr->drift_detector = drift_detector;\n\n    pthread_mutex_init(&pmgr->state_mtx, NULL);\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"PipelineManager '%s' (v%s) created\", pmgr->name, PIPELINE_VERSION);\n    return pmgr;\n}\n\nvoid pipeline_manager_destroy(pipeline_manager_t *pmgr)\n{\n    if (!pmgr) return;\n\n    pipeline_manager_stop(pmgr);\n\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n\n    if (pmgr->ingestor)\n        data_ingestor_destroy(pmgr->ingestor);\n\n    /* The factory, registry and detector are owned by caller (DI container) */\n    free(pmgr);\n    LOG_INFO(\"PipelineManager destroyed\");\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                      Public API — execution lifecycle                      *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nbool pipeline_manager_start(pipeline_manager_t *pmgr)\n{\n    if (!pmgr) return false;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n    if (pmgr->running) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n        LOG_WARN(\"PipelineManager already running\");\n        return true;\n    }\n    pmgr->running = true;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n\n    /* Spawn background threads -------------------------------------------------- */\n    if (pthread_create(&pmgr->ingestion_thr, NULL, ingestion_loop, pmgr) != 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to spawn ingestion thread: %s\", strerror(errno));\n        pmgr->running = false;\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (pthread_create(&pmgr->monitor_thr, NULL, monitor_loop, pmgr) != 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to spawn drift monitor thread: %s\", strerror(errno));\n        pmgr->running = false;\n        pthread_cancel(pmgr->ingestion_thr);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"PipelineManager '%s' started\", pmgr->name);\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid pipeline_manager_stop(pipeline_manager_t *pmgr)\n{\n    if (!pmgr) return;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n    bool was_running = pmgr->running;\n    pmgr->running = false;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n\n    if (!was_running)\n        return;\n\n    /* Wait for threads to finish ------------------------------------------------ */\n    pthread_join(pmgr->ingestion_thr, NULL);\n    pthread_join(pmgr->monitor_thr,  NULL);\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"PipelineManager '%s' gracefully stopped\", pmgr->name);\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                      Public API — imperative operations                    *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nbool pipeline_manager_trigger_training(pipeline_manager_t *pmgr,\n                                       const char         *reason)\n{\n    if (!pmgr) return false;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n    bool ok = trigger_training_locked(pmgr, reason);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                         Internal helper implementations                    *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * ingestion_loop\n * --------------\n * Background thread that pulls LMS data at a constant cadence and stores the\n * results in the shared Feature Store.  If the DataIngestor detects anomalies\n * in the incoming stream, it automatically logs metrics to Prometheus via\n * util/logger.  Any ingestion error toggles a soft failure state; after three\n * consecutive failures, the pipeline issues an ALERT severity log entry.\n */\nstatic void *ingestion_loop(void *arg)\n{\n    pipeline_manager_t *pmgr = (pipeline_manager_t *)arg;\n    unsigned consecutive_failures = 0;\n\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Ingestion thread started\");\n    while (pmgr->running) {\n\n        if (data_ingestor_ingest(pmgr->ingestor) == 0) {\n            /* Success case ----------------------------------------------------- */\n            consecutive_failures = 0;\n            LOG_INFO(\"Ingestion successful\");\n        } else {\n            /* Failure handling -------------------------------------------------- */\n            consecutive_failures++;\n            LOG_ERROR(\"Ingestion failed (attempt #%u)\", consecutive_failures);\n            if (consecutive_failures >= 3)\n                LOG_ALERT(\"Consecutive ingestion failures >= 3 — check LMS connectivity\");\n        }\n\n        /* Re-schedule --------------------------------------------------------- */\n        safe_sleep_sec(INGESTION_POLL_SEC);\n    }\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Ingestion thread exiting\");\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/**\n * monitor_loop\n * ------------\n * Observer Pattern consumer that listens for drift_detector notifications.\n * When drift is detected, the loop triggers a retraining job via the Factory.\n */\nstatic void *monitor_loop(void *arg)\n{\n    pipeline_manager_t *pmgr = (pipeline_manager_t *)arg;\n\n    /* Subscribe to drift events ------------------------------------------------- */\n    drift_event_handle_t handle =\n        drift_detector_subscribe(pmgr->drift_detector);\n\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Drift-monitor thread started\");\n    while (pmgr->running) {\n\n        drift_event_t event;\n        if (drift_detector_await_event(handle, &event, 30 /* sec timeout */) < 0)\n            continue; /* timeout or error */\n\n        if (event.type == DRIFT_EVENT_TYPE_DRIFT_DETECTED) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"Data or model drift detected => triggering retraining\");\n            pthread_mutex_lock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n            bool ok = trigger_training_locked(pmgr, \"drift-detected\");\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&pmgr->state_mtx);\n\n            if (!ok)\n                LOG_ERROR(\"Monitor loop retraining request failed\");\n\n            safe_sleep_sec(RETRAINING_BACKOFF_SEC);\n        }\n    }\n\n    drift_detector_unsubscribe(handle);\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Drift-monitor thread exiting\");\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/**\n * trigger_training_locked\n * -----------------------\n * Helper that assumes the caller already holds pmgr->state_mtx.  It builds\n * a TrainingJob via the factory, pushes metadata to the Model Registry, then\n * executes the job synchronously.  Production deployments might execute the\n * job asynchronously on a Kubernetes back-end; the synchronous approach is\n * used here for brevity.\n *\n * Return true on success, false otherwise.\n */\nstatic bool trigger_training_locked(pipeline_manager_t *pmgr,\n                                    const char         *reason)\n{\n    /* Build the Training Job -------------------------------------------------- */\n    training_job_t *job =\n        training_job_factory_create(pmgr->job_factory, reason);\n\n    if (!job) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"training_job_factory_create failed\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    char exp_id[MODEL_REGISTRY_MAX_ID];\n    if (model_registry_create_experiment(pmgr->registry,\n                                         job,\n                                         reason,\n                                         exp_id,\n                                         sizeof(exp_id)) < 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to log experiment into Model Registry\");\n        training_job_destroy(job);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Experiment '%s' registered, launching training job\", exp_id);\n\n    /* Execute the training job ------------------------------------------------ */\n    if (training_job_execute(job) != 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Training job execution failed for experiment '%s'\", exp_id);\n        model_registry_update_status(pmgr->registry, exp_id, MODEL_STATUS_FAILED);\n        training_job_destroy(job);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Persist success status to registry ------------------------------------- */\n    if (model_registry_update_status(pmgr->registry,\n                                     exp_id,\n                                     MODEL_STATUS_SUCCEEDED) != 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Unable to update experiment '%s' status in registry\", exp_id);\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Training completed successfully for experiment '%s'\", exp_id);\n    training_job_destroy(job);\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                            Utility functions                               *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic void safe_sleep_sec(unsigned seconds)\n{\n    struct timespec req  = { .tv_sec  = seconds, .tv_nsec = 0 };\n    while (nanosleep(&req, &req) == -1 && errno == EINTR)\n        ; /* Restart if interrupted by signal */\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                              Version info                                  *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nconst char *pipeline_manager_version(void)\n{\n    return PIPELINE_VERSION;\n}","numLines":359,"startLine":1,"totalLines":359}}}

The registry tracks the full\n *      life-cycle of ML models—versioning, stage promotion, metadata,\n *      and lineage—for reproducible research and robust MLOps.\n *\n *  Usage:\n *      #include \"model_registry.h\"\n *\n *      if (ml_registry_init(\"/var/lib/lexilearn/registry\") != ML_OK)\n *          die(ml_registry_last_error());\n *\n *      char model_id[ML_MODEL_ID_MAX] = {0};\n *      ml_registry_register_model(\"hybrid-summarizer\",\n *                                 \"v1.3.4\",\n *                                 \"/models/hybrid-summarizer_v1.3.4.bin\",\n *                                 \"{\\\"lr\\\":0.0001, \\\"epochs\\\":12}\",\n *                                 \"{\\\"val_bleu\\\":0.87}\",\n *                                 \"b01cbe6\",\n *                                 model_id,\n *                                 sizeof(model_id));\n *\n *      ml_registry_shutdown();\n */\n\n#ifndef LEXILEARN_MODEL_REGISTRY_H\n#define LEXILEARN_MODEL_REGISTRY_H\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  System Headers                                                           */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#include <stddef.h>     /* size_t      */\n#include <stdint.h>     /* uint64_t    */\n#include <time.h>       /* time_t      */\n#include <stdio.h>      /* FILE        */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Feature Flags / Config                                                   */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n#ifndef ML_REGISTRY_THREAD_SAFE\n#define ML_REGISTRY_THREAD_SAFE 1\n#endif\n\n/* Maximum byte length for a model identifier (“{strategy}:{hash}”)          */\n#define ML_MODEL_ID_MAX          128\n\n/* JSON blobs are stored in TEXT columns – reasonable upper bound            */\n#define ML_JSON_MAX              8192\n\n/* Strategy name ≤ 64 chars; version tag ≤ 32 chars                          */\n#define ML_STRATEGY_MAX          64\n#define ML_VERSION_TAG_MAX       32\n\n/* Internal filesystem limits                                               */\n#define ML_PATH_MAX              4096\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Error Handling                                                           */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    ML_OK = 0,\n    ML_ERR_INIT          = -1,\n    ML_ERR_SHUTDOWN      = -2,\n    ML_ERR_IO            = -3,\n    ML_ERR_MEMORY        = -4,\n    ML_ERR_INVALID_ARG   = -5,\n    ML_ERR_NOT_FOUND     = -6,\n    ML_ERR_CONFLICT      = -7,\n    ML_ERR_SERIALIZATION = -8,\n    ML_ERR_UNKNOWN       = -100\n} ml_error_t;\n\n/**\n * Returns a thread-local, NULL-terminated string describing the last error.\n * The pointer is owned by the registry and MUST NOT be freed by the caller.\n */\nconst char *ml_registry_last_error(void);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Model Metadata Structures                                                */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/* Model stage / life-cycle gates                                            */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    ML_STAGE_EXPERIMENT = 0,  /* Default after registration                 */\n    ML_STAGE_STAGING    = 1,  /* Candidate awaiting production sign-off     */\n    ML_STAGE_PRODUCTION = 2,  /* Actively serving recommendations           */\n    ML_STAGE_ARCHIVED   = 3   /* Frozen for audit or rollback               */\n} ml_model_stage_t;\n\n/* Primary key is a deterministic string: <strategy>::<git-sha>              */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char             model_id[ML_MODEL_ID_MAX];   /* PK                     */\n    char             strategy[ML_STRATEGY_MAX];   /* Strategy pattern key   */\n    char             version_tag[ML_VERSION_TAG_MAX]; /* “v1.2.3”           */\n    char             artifact_path[ML_PATH_MAX];  /* Path to binary / pkl   */\n\n    /* JSON blobs: hyper-parameters, metrics, & additional metadata          */\n    char             hyperparams_json[ML_JSON_MAX];\n    char             metrics_json[ML_JSON_MAX];\n\n    char             git_commit_sha[16];          /* short SHA (7–15 chars) */\n    ml_model_stage_t stage;                       /* Current stage          */\n\n    time_t           created_at;                  /* Registration timestamp */\n    time_t           last_updated;                /* Stage promotion, etc.  */\n\n} ml_model_record_t;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Registry Lifecycle                                                       */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Initializes / opens the registry at the given root directory.\n * This call is idempotent.  Multiple opens simply bump an internal ref-count.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   registry_root – absolute path to directory containing registry.db & blobs\n *\n * Returns:\n *   ML_OK on success, negative error code otherwise.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_init(const char *registry_root);\n\n/**\n * Flushes pending IO and releases resources.  Balanced with ml_registry_init().\n * The registry may remain open if other references are active.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_shutdown(void);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  CRUD – Create                                                            */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Registers a new model artifact & metadata into the registry.  Function is\n * atomic; either all metadata are flushed to disk or an error is returned.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   strategy_name     – logical family (e.g., “transformer-summarizer”)\n *   version_tag       – human-readable tag (“v2.1-alpha”)\n *   artifact_path     – absolute / relative path to binary artifact\n *   hyperparams_json  – serialized hyperparameters (may be NULL / \"\")\n *   metrics_json      – serialized evaluation metrics (may be NULL / \"\")\n *   git_commit_sha    – optional SCM commit for code–model lineage\n *   out_model_id      – buffer where unique model_id will be written\n *   out_model_id_len  – size of the above buffer\n *\n * Returns:\n *   ML_OK on success, error code otherwise.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_register_model(const char *strategy_name,\n                                      const char *version_tag,\n                                      const char *artifact_path,\n                                      const char *hyperparams_json,\n                                      const char *metrics_json,\n                                      const char *git_commit_sha,\n                                      char       *out_model_id,\n                                      size_t      out_model_id_len);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  CRUD – Read                                                              */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Retrieves a model record by ID.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   model_id   – composite key returned by ml_registry_register_model\n *   out_record – caller-allocated pointer; filled on success\n *\n * Returns:\n *   ML_OK or ML_ERR_NOT_FOUND.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_get_model(const char *model_id,\n                                 ml_model_record_t *out_record);\n\n/**\n * Returns the latest (by created_at) model for a given strategy & stage.\n * Useful when Controllers need “latest stable” automatically.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   strategy_name – e.g., “n_gram_analyzer”\n *   desired_stage – ML_STAGE_STAGING / ML_STAGE_PRODUCTION, etc.\n *   out_model_id  – buffer to receive the model_id\n *   len           – bytes available in buffer\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_get_latest_model(const char    *strategy_name,\n                                        ml_model_stage_t desired_stage,\n                                        char           *out_model_id,\n                                        size_t          len);\n\n/**\n * Lists all models for a given strategy.  The registry allocates an array that\n * the caller MUST free via ml_registry_free_model_list().\n *\n * Parameters:\n *   strategy_name – filter; NULL for all strategies\n *   out_list      – on success, *out_list points to malloc-ed array\n *   out_count     – number of elements in array\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_list_models(const char  *strategy_name,\n                                   ml_model_record_t **out_list,\n                                   size_t *out_count);\n\n/**\n * Releases memory returned by ml_registry_list_models().\n */\nvoid ml_registry_free_model_list(ml_model_record_t *list);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Update                                                                   */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Promotes / demotes a model to a new stage (e.g., STAGING → PRODUCTION).\n * Audit information is automatically appended in an internal changelog.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_set_stage(const char *model_id, ml_model_stage_t new_stage);\n\n/**\n * Adds / overwrites metrics for an existing model, used by continuous eval.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_update_metrics(const char *model_id,\n                                      const char *metrics_json);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Delete / Garbage Collection                                              */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Archives a model and optionally removes its artifact from disk to reclaim\n * storage.  For compliance, metadata are retained unless hard_delete is true.\n */\nml_error_t ml_registry_archive_model(const char *model_id, int hard_delete);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Observability                                                            */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * Exports a read-only FILE* handle to the registry’s change-feed, suitable for\n * tail-f style monitoring or integration with Observer Pattern hooks that\n * trigger automated retraining.\n *\n * Note: Returned FILE* is shared; do NOT fclose(). A small\n *      registry implemented in model_strategy.c maps names to constructors,\n *      allowing educators and researchers to swap algorithms without touching\n *      Controller code or recompiling the full orchestrator.\n *\n *  Notes:\n *      • All functions return lexi_status_t for uniform error handling.\n *      • The interface is intentionally agnostic to the underlying ML\n *        framework (ONNX, TorchScript, custom C kernels, etc.).\n *      • This file is pure C and therefore includes an extern \"C\" guard so\n *        that strategies may be implemented in C++ when convenient.\n *\n *  Author:  LexiLearn Engineering\n *  License: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef LEXILEARN_MODEL_STRATEGY_H\n#define LEXILEARN_MODEL_STRATEGY_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>   /* size_t */\n#include <stdint.h>   /* int64_t */\n#include <stdbool.h>  /* bool */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Global status / error-code enumeration\n *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef enum\n{\n    LEXI_OK                    =  0,\n    LEXI_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT  = -1,\n    LEXI_ERR_NO_MEMORY         = -2,\n    LEXI_ERR_IO                = -3,\n    LEXI_ERR_NOT_IMPLEMENTED   = -4,\n    LEXI_ERR_INTERNAL          = -5\n} lexi_status_t;\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Task types supported by LexiLearn.  Used for routing to the right model.\n *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef enum\n{\n    TASK_SUMMARIZATION = 0,\n    TASK_GRAMMAR_CORRECTION,\n    TASK_PROFICIENCY_SCORING,\n    TASK_KEYWORD_EXTRACTION,\n    TASK_CUSTOM\n} lexi_task_t;\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Generic hyper-parameter container\n *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef enum\n{\n    HP_TYPE_INT,\n    HP_TYPE_DOUBLE,\n    HP_TYPE_BOOL,\n    HP_TYPE_STRING\n} hp_type_t;\n\ntypedef struct\n{\n    const char *key;    /* e.g. \"learning_rate\" */\n    hp_type_t   type;\n    union\n    {\n        int64_t     i;\n        double      d;\n        bool        b;\n        const char *s;\n    } value;\n} hyperparam_t;\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Forward declarations of opaque resource types used across the system\n *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef struct ll_dataset_t        ll_dataset_t;        /* defined in dataset.h */\ntypedef struct ll_feature_store_t  ll_feature_store_t;  /* defined in feature_store.h */\ntypedef struct ll_model_artifact_t ll_model_artifact_t; /* defined in registry.h */\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Strategy object & vtable\n *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\nstruct model_strategy_vtable; /* fwd */\n\ntypedef struct model_strategy\n{\n    const char *name;                           /* strategy identifier */\n    const struct model_strategy_vtable *vptr;   /* method table        */\n    void *impl;                                 /* strategy-specific data */\n} model_strategy_t;\n\n/*\n *  Virtual table defining the lifecycle of a model strategy.\n *\n *  Implementations MUST fill in all mandatory function pointers.  Optional\n *  operations (e.g. load/save) may return LEXI_ERR_NOT_IMPLEMENTED when\n *  unsupported.\n */\ntypedef struct model_strategy_vtable\n{\n    /*\n     *  configure():\n     *      Apply a list of hyper-parameters prior to training.\n     *\n     *  Return:\n     *      LEXI_OK on success, or appropriate error code.\n     */\n    lexi_status_t (*configure)(model_strategy_t      *self,\n                               const hyperparam_t    *params,\n                               size_t                 param_count);\n\n    /*\n     *  train():\n     *      Fit the model to the given datasets.  The model artefact emitted\n     *      here is registered with the Model Registry by the Controller.\n     */\n    lexi_status_t (*train)(model_strategy_t      *self,\n                           const ll_dataset_t    *training_data,\n                           const ll_dataset_t    *validation_data,\n                           ll_model_artifact_t  **out_model);\n\n    /*\n     *  predict():\n     *      Run inference using the current state of the model.\n     *      Predictions are returned as a dataset so that downstream pipeline\n     *      stages can treat them uniformly.\n     */\n    lexi_status_t (*predict)(model_strategy_t   *self,\n                             const ll_dataset_t *input_data,\n                             ll_dataset_t      **out_predictions);\n\n    /*\n     *  evaluate():\n     *      Compute an evaluation metric (e.g. BLEU, ROUGE-L, accuracy).\n     *      Metric choice is strategy-specific.\n     */\n    lexi_status_t (*evaluate)(model_strategy_t   *self,\n                              const ll_dataset_t *test_data,\n                              double             *out_score);\n\n    /*\n     *  save() / load():\n     *      Persist or restore the model.  Implementation may stream to/from\n     *      cloud object storage, local disk, or a database URI.\n     */\n    lexi_status_t (*save)(model_strategy_t *self,\n                          const char       *artifact_uri);\n\n    lexi_status_t (*load)(model_strategy_t *self,\n                          const char       *artifact_uri);\n\n    /*\n     *  destroy():\n     *      Finaliser invoked by the orchestrator when the strategy is no\n     *      longer needed.  Must free all memory and release GPU/CPU handles.\n     */\n    void (*destroy)(model_strategy_t *self);\n\n} model_strategy_vtable_t;\n\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Registry API\n *  – Facilitates dynamic discovery and instantiation of strategies\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/* Prototype that concrete strategies must expose */\ntypedef model_strategy_t *(*strategy_ctor_fn)(void);\n\n/*\n *  ll_strategy_register():\n *      Register a constructor under a unique, case-insensitive name.\n *\n *  Typical usage (inside transformer_summarizer.c):\n *      static model_strategy_t *transformer_ctor(void) { … }\n *      __attribute__((constructor))\n *      static void _init(void)\n *      {\n *          ll_strategy_register(\"transformer_summarizer\", transformer_ctor);\n *      }\n */\nlexi_status_t ll_strategy_register(const char      *name,\n                                   strategy_ctor_fn ctor);\n\n/*\n *  ll_strategy_create():\n *      Instantiate a strategy by name.  Caller owns the returned pointer and\n *      must eventually invoke ->vptr->destroy().\n */\nmodel_strategy_t *ll_strategy_create(const char *name);\n\n/*\n *  ll_strategy_list():\n *      Enumerate all names currently registered.
